Let's write helper:
```
def add_position_coeff(h, p, scale, out):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1
    if scale ==0 or n==1: return
    out[mid] += scale*(1.0-2.0*h[p])
    if p > 0:
        out[mid+1:mid+p+1] += scale*(1.0 - h[p-1::-1])
        out[mid-1:mid-p-1:-1] += scale*(-h[p-1::-1])
    if p < n-1:
        ln = n-p-1
        out[mid+1:mid+ln+1] += scale*(-h[p+1:])
        # neg1: indices mid-1 down to p inclusive
        stop = p-1
        if stop < 0:
            out[mid-1::-1] += scale*(1.0 - h[p+1:])
        else:
            out[mid-1:stop:-1] += scale*(1.0 - h[p+1:])
```
Need check negative second slice stop: For p>0: out[mid-1:mid-p-1:-1]. If p=n-1? Then stop=mid-(n-1)-1=mid-n=-1? But p<n? For p=n-1, h[p-1::-1] length n-1. Negative second m=1..n-1: indices mid-1..0. stop=mid-p-1 = (n-1)-(n-1)-1=-1. `out[mid-1:-1:-1]`? Using previous rule, with p>0 but stop=-1, to include index0 should use None, not -1. ```
stop = mid - p - 1

Candidate generation:
We can implement a powerful `fast_local_search(h,c,deadline,rng)`:
- Input h and c exact. n.
- best_c = max(c). best_h = h.copy(); best_c_scaled = best_c.
- Use orientation-aware pair moves.
Algorithm idea:
For passes until no improvement/time:
  - Sort lags by current c descending. Consider top K lags (maybe 8 + also symmetric? already both). For each lag index t in top K:
    m=abs(t-mid)
    if m==0: Can't reduce with transfers? Lag0 is ∑h(1-h) affected by any move; can reduce by moving toward 0/1. But local binary maybe c0 small. We can handle separately by binarization/binary local.
    Determine direction: For t > mid (positive): q_t = ∑ h_{i+m}(1-h_i). To reduce, move from dst? source = i+m (later), target = i (earlier). For each i=0..n-m-1: src=i+m, dst=i. If h[src] > h[dst], candidate d in [0,min(h[src],1-h[dst])]. If h<=, moving reverse may increase q_t but could reduce other lags; maybe not for primary greedy. We can allow if line search improves global max.
    For t < mid (negative): q_t = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+m}). Source=i (earlier), target=i+m. Move from source to target if h[src] > h[dst].
  For each candidate, run lineSearch (grid + maybe ternary) to find d improving current global max. Apply best move across all candidates? Could greedily apply first improvement or best.
Because objective convex along each move, if a move reduces current max, we can apply maximum improvement. Maybe apply the candidate with largest decrease (or any) and update. Repeat.
This is like coordinate descent solving active constraints.
Limit evaluations by top K and maybe all i for each. If n=500, K=8 -> 4000 candidates per pass. Each candidate line search 6-8 d max ~8*1000=8k ops; 4000 ->32M ops per pass, maybe 0.2s? In Python slice overhead bigger. Maybe 1-2s per pass. 10 passes 10s. Good. Could do more.

Fast update:
```
def add_position_coeff(h, p, scale, out):
    n = len(h)
    mid = n-1
    out[mid] += scale*(1.0 - 2.0*h[p])
    if p:
        h_slice = h[p-1::-1]
        # pos first
        out[mid+1:mid+p+1] += scale*(1.0 - h_slice)
        # neg second
        stop = mid - p - 1
        if stop < 0:
            stop = None
        out[mid-1:stop:-1] += scale*(-h_slice)
    if p < n-1:
        tail = h[p+1:]
        ln = len(tail)
        out[mid+1:mid+ln+1] += scale*(-tail)
        stop = p-1
        if stop < 0:
            out[mid-1::-1] += scale*(1.0 - tail)
        else:
            out[mid-1:stop:-1] += scale*(1.0 - tail)
```
Check lengths for negative first p=0: tail length n-1, out[mid-1::-1] length mid = n-1? mid=n-1, start n-2, includes n-1 elements? length = n-1. good. For p>0: tail length n-p-1, slice start mid-1=n-2, stop=p-1, length = (n-2) - (p-1) = n-p-1? Example p=1,n=6: start4 stop0 length4 = n-p-1 yes. Good.
For neg second p>0: h_slice length p. stop=mid-p-1=n-1-p-1=n-p-2. start mid-1=n-2. Length if stop>=0: (n-2) - (n-p-2)=p. If stop<0: None length n-1? For p=n-1: stop=-2? n-p-2=-1? Wait p=n-1: mid-p-1 = n-1-(n-1)-1=-1. We set None. start n-2, slice `out[n-2::-1]` length n-1. h_slice length n-1. good. If stop= -? For p=n-2: stop=n-(n-2)-2=0, slice start n-2 stop0 length n-2? h_slice length n-2? p=n-2, length p=n-2. good. For p=1,n=3: stop=2-1-1=0, slice out[1:0:-1] length1, h length1. good. For p=0, skip.
Positive second: out[mid+1:mid+ln+1] length ln; tail length ln. Good.
Positive first: length p.

Line search function:
```
def line_search_move(h, c, src, dst, cmax, dx, grid=None):
    n=len(h); lim = min(h[src], 1-h[dst])
    if lim <= 1e-12: return 0, None
    a = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    add_position_coeff(h, dst, dx, a)
    add_position_coeff(h, src, -dx, a)
    m = abs(dst-src)
    idx1 = 0; idx2=-1
    if m>0:
        mid=n-1; idx1=mid+m; idx2=mid-m
    best_d=0; best_val=cmax
    # choose grid based on lim
    # include sorted unique fractions
    grid = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.75,0.9,1.0]
    # also lim*some min
    vals=[]
    for f in grid:
       d=f*lim
       if d<=1e-12: continue
       # optional skip if d > lim
       tmp = c + a*d
       if m:
          dd=d*d
          tmp[idx1] += dd
          tmp[idx2] += dd
       val = np.max(tmp)
       if val < best_val - 1e-14:
          best_val=val; best_d=d
    # If best at max fraction, maybe try boundary lim (if grid 1.0 included) already.
    # If best at 0.05, maybe try smaller? Could add 0.02.
    if best_d > 0 and best_d/lim <= 0.15:
       for f in [0.02,0.03,0.04]: ...
    if best_d >0 and best_d/lim >0.85:
       # grid likely 1.0 best; maybe interior if not, grid enough.
    return best_d, a, best_val? 
```

def fast_local_search(h, c, cmax, deadline, top_k=10, max_passes=200, recompute_every=50, accept_tol=1e-13, min_delta=1e-6):
  n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2/n; L=2n-1
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.max(); best_cmax=best_c
  moves_since_recomp=0
  improved_passes=0
  while time<deadline-...:
    c = compute_c_all(h,n,dx) maybe at start of each pass? We can maintain but recompute each pass to get lags. Cost cheap.
    cmax = c.max()
    order = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    best_candidate = None
    for t in order:
      m = abs(t-mid)
      if m==0: continue
      sign = 1 if t>mid else -1
      # for i range, source,target
      # if sign positive: source=i+m, target=i
      for i in range(0,n-m):
         if sign>0: src=i+m; dst=i
         else: src=i; dst=i+m
         ss=h[src]; dt=h[dst]
         if ss <= dt + 1e-8: continue
         lim = min(ss, 1-dt)
         if lim < 1e-6: continue
         # cheap: derivative active = dt - ss - 1 < -1 - eps? since ss>dt, yes
         d,a,val=... line_search
         if d>0 and val < cmax - 1e-13:
            if best_candidate is None or val < best_candidate_val:
               save (src,dst,d,a,val)
            # Could apply immediately to reduce many, but best across pass may be better. Applying first may waste. We can collect if cost.
  if best_candidate:
     apply moves, update h,c; moves++; if moves%recompute==0 recompute
     best update if cmax improves? We need current c after move equals val for best? The val from line search is max after move using old c exact. If we apply, c updated exact. But if c had drift, recompute.
  else:
     # try random perturbation / SA
```

Now implement `steepest_pass(h,c,cmax,deadline)`:
For each t in top lags:
  sign determined. For i in range(n-m):
    if sign positive: src=i+m, dst=i

    if h[src] <= h[dst] + skip_tol: continue
    lim = min(h[src], 1-h[dst])
    if lim < skip_tol: continue
    # line search limited to a candidate a? We can pre-evaluate max possible reduction of this active lag: derivative of q_t = h_src - h_dst -1 (negative). If very small (source-target tiny), improvement limited; skip if h_src-h_dst < min_gap maybe.
    d,a,val,cross = line_search...
    track best val and move.
After pass, apply best.

def line_search_move(h,c,src,dst,cmax, dx, grid=None, min_improve=1e-14):

          ...

Pseudo `fast_local_search`:
```
def fast_local_search(h, c, cmax, deadline, top_k=8, max_passes=1000, recompute_interval=20, grid_size=None):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=max(c)
    h=h.copy(); c=c.copy()
    passes=0; no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
        # recompute start maybe every pass? To get lags and control drift
        if passes % recompute_interval ==0:
            c = compute_all_lags(h,n,dx); cmax = float(c.max())
        else:
            cmax = max(best_current?) # we have c and cmax after move exact; but top lags may drift if no recompute? c updated exact, cmax exact except round; okay.
        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
        best_move=None; best_val=cmax
        for t in order:
           m=abs(t-mid); if m==0 continue
           sign = 1 if t>mid else -1
           # if sign positive: src=i+m, dst=i; else src=i,dst=i+m
           step = ...
           for i in range(0,n-m):
              src = i+m if sign>0 else i; dst = i if sign>0 else i+m
              if h[src] <= h[dst] + 1e-10: continue
              lim = min(h[src], 1-h[dst])
              if lim <= 1e-10: continue
              # estimate active derivative: if dt - ss -1? always < -1? Actually ss>dt, derivative of active = dt - ss -1 < -1 - eps? if ss-dt>0, yes <=-1. Wait if ss=0.6,dt=0.5 derivative=-1.1. Good. Not skip.
              d, val, a = line_search_move(..., cmax, dx=dx, cmax)
              if d>0 and val < best_val - 1e-13:
                   best_val=val; best_move=(src,dst,d,a)
        if best_move:
           src,dst,d,a=best_move
           apply_move(h,c,src,dst,d,dx,a)
           if best_val + 1e-15 < best_c:
               best_c=best_val; best_h=h.copy()  # c may not recompute but exact
           no_improve=0
           passes+=1
        else:
           no_improve+=1
           # plateau: maybe random perturb and local? We'll handle outside? Inside could do few SA moves.
           if no_improve > 5 and time...:
                # random small perturb around current, if improves best? We can try global best restart later.
                break
    # final recompute best
    c_best=compute_all_lags(best_h,n,dx); cbest=max
    return best_h, cbest
```

Implement `project_feasible(h,n)`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1).astype(float)
  target=n/2.0
  for it in range(100):
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    if diff>0:
       room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
       if tot<1e-15: break
       h += (diff/tot)*room
    else:
       tot=h.sum()
       if tot<1e-15: break
       h += (diff/tot)*h
  # final exact adjust: if sum diff, find index with room and set
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
     # move small from/to many? Use above one more
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
But if after clip, sum may not exactly. Use a final distributed adjust: if diff>0, add to indices with h<1 proportional; if exact impossible due rounding, okay. Need ensure not violate. Could use while with residual.

For transfers, sum preserved exactly in floating? `h[src]-=d; h[dst]+=d`; due rounding sum may change by eps. Over many moves maybe drift ~1e-12. Use periodic project (especially before returning). But project can change objective slightly, may increase best. We can project and recompute; if worsens, maybe keep unprojected if within feasibility tolerance. The harness likely checks sum with tolerance. We can make sum very close. Use `enforce_sum` by adjusting an index not at boundary by diff; if no room, proportional. Implement `exact_project` after each recompute? For fast local, projection can break exact c; do only if residual >1e-12. Since transfers preserve sum, residual small. `ensure_sum(h,n)`:
```
def ensure_sum(h,n):
  target=n/2
  diff=target - h.sum()
  if abs(diff) < 1e-14: return h
  # distribute among indices where can move in right direction without hitting bounds; if diff positive, room=1-h with room>eps; else floor=h>eps
  # add diff*weight/sum to reduce diff exactly
  for _ in range(3):
     ...
  # final single coordinate adjust if small
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
     # find candidate with room sign
     if diff>0:
       idxs=np.where(h<1-1e-12)[0]
     else: idxs=np.where(h>1e-12)[0]
     if len: idx=idxs[np.argmin(...)] ; h[idx] = clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
  return h
```
But modifying one index by diff may violate if diff > room; use proportional.

```
def binary_swap_search(h, c, cmax, deadline, top_k=12, max_passes=5000, recompute=100):
  # h exact 0/1 sum n/2
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
  while time<deadline:
    # recompute occasionally
    c=compute_all_lags(h,n,dx); cmax=max
    if cmax<best_c: best...
    order=top lags
    best_move=None; best_val=cmax
    for t in order:
      m, sign
      for i:
        src,dst
        if h[src]==1 and h[dst]==0:
           val,a=evaluate_full_swap
           if val < best_val - 1e-15: store
    if best_move: apply
       best update
    else:
       # try random 2-swap or accept? break
       if random perturb? We'll break.
  final.
```

4. `binarize(h,n,seed=None,sym=False)`:
```
def binarize(h,n,sym=False):
  h=np.asarray(h,float)
  out=np.zeros(n)
  if sym:
    # n even
    pair_vals=np.empty(n//2)
    for i in range(n//2): pair_vals[i]=0.5*(h[i]+h[n-1-i])
    top=np.argsort(pair_vals)[::-1][:n//4]
    out[totop]=1; out[n-1-totop]=1
  else:
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n//2]
    out[idx]=1
  return out
```
For n maybe not divisible by4? We only choose divisible by4 for sym. If n not, skip sym.

`move_effect`:
```
def move_effect(h,c,src,dst,d,dx,want_a=True):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1; mid=n-1
    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float) if want_a else None
    if want_a:
       add_position_coeff(h,dst,dx,a); add_position_coeff(h,src,-dx,a)
    # compute val without a? Need if not want_a maybe not used.
    tmp = c.copy()
    if want_a:
       tmp += a*d
    else: return None,None
    tmp[mid] -= 2.0*dx*d*d
    m=abs(dst-src)
    if m>0:
       add=dx*d*d
       tmp[mid+m] += add
       tmp[mid-m] += add
    val=float(tmp.max())
    return val,a
```
For full swap d=1. For line search, building tmp for each d expensive; we can separate.

Line search:
```
def line_search_move(h,c,src,dst,cmax,dx):
    lim = min(h[src], 1-h[dst])
    if lim < 1e-12: return None
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; m=abs(src-dst); idxp=mid+m; idxn=mid-m
    a = move_a_vector(h,src,dst,dx)
    best_d=0; best_val=cmax + 1e-15
    # quick
    for f in QUICK_FR:
       d=f*lim
       if d<=1e-13: continue
       val=evaluate_move_with_a(c,a,d,dx,mid,idxp,idxn)
       if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_d=d
    if best_d ==0: return None (unless maybe very small no)
    # full
    for f in FULL_FR: ...
    return best_d,best_val,a
```

Now, `move_a_vector(h,src,dst,dx)` top-level:
```
def move_a_vector(h,src,dst,dx):
    L=2*len(h)-1
    a=np.zeros(L)
    add_position_coeff(h,dst,dx,a)
    add_position_coeff(h,src,-dx,a)
    return a
```

Apply move:
```
def apply_move(h,c,src,dst,d,dx,a):
    mid=len(h)-1; m=abs(dst-src)
    c += a*d
    c[mid] -= 2.0*dx*d*d
    add=dx*d*d
    if m>0:
       c[mid+m] += add
       c[mid-m] += add
    h[src] -= d
    h[dst] += d
    # clip tiny
    if h[src] < 0 and h[src] > -1e-12: h[src]=0
    if h[dst] >1 and h[dst] < 1+1e-12: h[dst]=1
```
Need if d saturates, h exact maybe.

def apply_full_swap_binary(h,c,src,dst,dx,a):
   c += a; c[mid]-=2dx; ...
   h[src]=0; h[dst]=1
```

`binary_search` details:
```
def binary_swap_search(h, c, cmax, deadline, top_k=12, max_passes=10000):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2/n
    # ensure h binary
    h=np.where(h>0.5,1.0,0.0)
    # ensure sum n/2? after binarize yes. If not, fix? skip.
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=float('inf')
    passes=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
       c = compute_all_lags(h,n)
       cmax = float(np.max(c))
       if cmax < best_c:
          best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       order=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
       best_move=None; best_val=cmax; best_a=None
       for t in order:
          m=abs(t-mid)
          if m==0: continue
          sign=1 if t>mid else -1
          for i in range(n-m):
             src = i+m if sign>0 else i
             dst = i if sign>0 else i+m
             if h[src] == 0.0 or h[dst] == 1.0: continue
             # src 1 dst 0
             # quick local derivative? active lag derivative = 1? Always negative? hsrc=1,hdst=0 => q active term old=1, swap reduces q_t by? new 0? Yes.
             idxp=mid+m; idxn=mid-m
             a=move_a_vector(h,src,dst,dx)
             val = evaluate_move_with_a(c,a,1.0,dx,mid,idxp,idxn)
             if val < best_val - 1e-15:
                best_val=val; best_move=(src,dst); best_a=a
       if best_move:
          src,dst=best_move
          apply_full_swap_binary(h,c,src,dst,dx,best_a)
          cmax_val = best_val # exact
          if cmax_val < best_c:
             best_c=cmax_val; best_h=h.copy()
          passes+=1
       else:
          break
    # final recompute best_h?
    c_best=compute_all_lags(best_h,n); cb=max
    return best_h, cb
```
This recomputes c every pass, costly? Correlate n=500 each pass, if thousands passes maybe 1000*250k=250M C, okay maybe seconds. We can implement `fast_local_search` witha parameter `max_passes` and use time. It should update c exactly and recompute periodically.

Pseudo:
```
def fast_local_search(h,c,cmax,deadline,top_k=None,max_passes=2000,recompute=50):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2/n
    if top_k is None: top_k = 16 if n<=600 else 10 if n<=1000 else 8
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    h=h.copy(); c=c.copy()
    passes=0; no=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2 and passes < max_passes:
       if passes % recompute == 0:
           c=compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax < best_c:
           best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       order = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
       best_move=None; best_val=cmax; best_a=None
       for t in order:
           m=abs(t-mid)
           if m==0 or time.time()>deadline: continue
           sign=1 if t>mid else -1
           for i in range(n-m):
               src = i+m if sign>0 else i
               dst = i if sign>0 else i+m
               ss=h[src]; dd=h[dst]
               if ss <= dd + 1e-9: continue  # only reduce this lag
               lim = ss if ss < 1-dd else 1-dd
               if lim <= 1e-9: continue
               res = line_search_move_fast(h,c,ss?...)
               if res and res[1] < best_val - 1e-14:
                    best_val=res[1]; best_move=(src,dst,res[0]); best_a=res[2]
       if best_move:
           src,dst,d=best_move
           apply_move(h,c,src,dst,d,dx,best_a)
           cmax=best_val
           if cmax < best_c: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
           no=0
       else:
           no +=1
           if no > 3:
               break
       passes +=1
    # final recompute best? 
    cb=compute_all_lags(best_h,n); cbm=...
    return best_h, cbm
```
Need pass `h` to line_search; `src,dst`. In line_search quick, we can use `ss,dd` for lim. But h values maybe changed? Not during candidate scan (current h). Good.

` line_search_move_fast ` should not copy h. It calls add coeff. We'll pass c, a.

```
def make_starts_from_best(best_h, n, seed, rng):
    starts=[]
    if len(best_h)==n:
       starts.append(best_h.copy())
       starts.append(project_feasible((best_h+best_h[::-1])/2,n))
    else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0,2,len(best_h)); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       h_int=np.interp(x_new,x_old,best_h); h_int=project_feasible(h_int,n); starts.append(h_int)
       h_sym=project_feasible((h_int+h_int[::-1])/2,n); starts.append(h_sym)
    # binary starts
    for s in starts:
       starts.append(binarize(s,n))
       if n%4==0: starts.append(binarize(s,n,sym=True))
    return starts
```

`binary_swap_search` details with time and recompute:
```
def binary_swap_search(h, c, cmax, deadline, top_k=12):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2.0/n
    h = np.where(h > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).astype(float)
    # ensure sum n/2? If not, fix by setting top? Might change; assume.
    c = compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    passes=0; recompute=50
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
        if passes % recompute == 0:
            c = compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        # top_k maybe if c small
        limit = top_k
        best_move=None; best_val=cmax; best_a=None
        for idx in range(min(limit, 2*n-1)):
            t = order[idx]
            m = abs(int(t)-mid)
            if m == 0: continue
            sign = 1 if t > mid else -1
            for i in range(0, n-m):
                src = i + m if sign > 0 else i
                dst = i if sign > 0 else i + m
                if h[src] != 1.0 or h[dst] != 0.0:
                    continue
                a = move_a_vector(h, src, dst, dx)
                val = evaluate_move_with_a(c, a, 1.0, dx, mid, mid+m, mid-m)
                if val < best_val - 1e-15:
                    best_val=val; best_move=(src,dst); best_a=a
            if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
        if best_move is not None:
            src,dst=best_move
            # apply
            c += best_a
            c[mid] -= 2.0*dx
            add=dx
            c[mid+m? need m variable from best; m can recompute abs(dst-src)
            h[src]=0.0; h[dst]=1.0
            cmax = best_val
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax; best_h = h.copy()
            passes +=1
        else:
            # final recompute current, compare
            c_cur = compute_all_lags(h,n); cur=float(c_cur.max())
            if cur < best_c:
                best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
            break
    c_cur=compute_all_lags(h,n); cur=...
    if cur < best_c: ...
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need `m` for apply: `m = abs(dst-src)`. Use best move m? Candidate m same. But if we recompute due best move with t? store m maybe. Store `best_m`. Or compute.
`c[mid+m] += dx`, `c[mid-m] += dx` if m>0. Since m>0.
Important: In apply, order c += best_a uses best_a from current h. Then h update. c cross. Good.

`fast_local_search` analogous. It should allow fractional h and not binarize. It should recompute c every e.g. 30 passes. It can maintain `cmax` as best_val after move; but if recompute, update. It should use `line_search_move` which includes quick/full.

`line_search_move` implementation top-level:
```
def line_search_move(h, c, src, dst, cmax, dx):
    ss = h[src]; td = h[dst]
    lim = ss
    room = 1.0 - td
    if room < lim: lim = room
    if lim <= 1e-10: return (0.0, cmax + 1.0, None)
    a = move_a_vector(h, src, dst, dx)
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; m=abs(src-dst); idxp=mid+m; idxn=mid-m
    best_d=0.0; best_val = cmax + 1e-13
    # quick fractions
    for f in QUICK_FR:
        d = f * lim
        if d <= 1e-12: continue
        val = evaluate_move_with_a(c, a, d, dx, mid, idxp, idxn)
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val; best_d = d
    if best_d == 0.0:
        return (0.0, best_val, None)
    # full fractions
    for f in FULL_FR:
       ... if val < best_val: ...
    return (best_d, best_val, a)
```
If cmax from current exact, requiring improvement >1e-14. If qick val is cmax - 1e-16, maybe skip. Use `best_val = cmax` and update only if `val < best_val - 1e-14`? But for tracking best across candidates, need maybe small. Use 1e-13 maybe. To find 1e-5 improvements, 1e-14 fine. However floating errors could cause false improvements of 1e-15. Use `IMPROVE = 1e-13`. Target 1e-5, okay. But binary exact values multiples dx, improvements multiples dx ~0.002; no issue. Fractional tiny maybe 1e-6, 1e-13 safe. Use 1e-13.
```
better = val < best_val - 1e-14
```
Initial best_val = cmax + 1e-12 maybe to require strict. But in pass, best_val=cmax. If val slightly below due error, maybe accept. Use `if val < best_val - 1e-14`.

`QUICK_FR` and `FULL_FR` as tuples top-level. If lim small, f*lim may underflow; check d>1e-12. If lim=1e-9, only d=1e-9? f=1.0; maybe too small. We can if lim <=1e-6 skip? Could miss tiny fractional. But tiny lim at bounds, max improvement O(lim^2) negligible. Use skip 1e-8.

Full search fractions should maybe include all quick. Use:
`QUICK_FR = (0.002, 0.01, 0.05, 0.25, 1.0)`
`FULL_FR = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8, 1.0)`
If best from quick at 1.0, full includes.

Now, `project_feasible` after noise/interp. Need robust. Let's code:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
        if diff > 0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            # add proportionally to room
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    # final correction on a few points
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       if diff > 0:
          idxs=np.where(h<1.0-1e-12)[0]
       else:
          idxs=np.where(h>1e-12)[0]
       if idxs.size:
          # distribute evenly among up to 1000
          pick=idxs[:min(1000,idxs.size)]
          add=diff/pick.size
          for idx in pick:
             if diff>0:
                add = min(add, 1.0-h[idx])
             else:
                add = max(add, -h[idx])? safer loop
          # Simpler: h[pick] += diff/pick.size; clip; residual ignore.
          h[pick] += diff/pick.size
          np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
```
The final h[pick] may still not exact and can violate if some hit bounds. But diff small. Could do iterative loop. For most cases, proportion loop enough exact to 1e-15. Because adding to room may push some to 1 but final clip; diff residual maybe. Use while:
```
for _ in range(10):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0: room=...; if tot... h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*room,0,1)
```
This can converge. We'll use similar to parent with 200 iterations. n up to 1200, okay.

`binarize`:
```
def binarize(h,n,sym=False):
    out=np.zeros(n)
    target=n//2
    if sym:
       if n % 4 !=0:
          # fallback non-sym
       else:
          pair_idx=np.arange(n//2)
          vals=0.5*(h[pair_idx]+h[n-1-pair_idx])
          top=np.argsort(vals)[::-1][:n//4]
          out[top]=1; out[n-1-top]=1
          return out
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:target]
    out[idx]=1
    return out
```
If h contains exactly .5 tie, arbitrary. Good.

def initial_fallback(n=500, seed=1):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   # start with triangular g.
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   g=1 - np.abs(x-1.0)  # edges0 center1? integral approx1? Actually trapezoid length2 height1 area1? triangle base2 height1 area1. Values at endpoints0, center1. This has mass1. Good.
   h=project_feasible(g,n)
   # Could add noise
   h=h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n)

Let's consider numerical correctness of add_position_coeff with slice stop. Need re-evaluate slice lengths to avoid mismatches that NumPy would error if lengths differ. Let's derive for each p carefully with exact formulas.

Let n, mid=n-1, L=2n-1.
Lag0: index mid.
Positive m=1..n-1: index mid+m.
Condition p first for positive m: p = a? Wait positive index q=∑ h_{i+m}(1-h_i). For position p to be first (h_p), need i+m=p => m=p-i, m in 1..p and i=p-m>=0. So m=1..p. Target indices mid+1..mid+p. Coeff for h_p first: 1 - h_{p-m} with m=1..p -> h[p-1], h[p-2],...,h[0] = h[p-1::-1]. Good.
Condition p second: i=p, m in 1..n-1-p. Target mid+1..mid+(n-1-p). Coeff derivative wrt h_p second: -h_{i+m} = -h[p+m], m=1..n-1-p -> h[p+1],...,h[n-1] = h[p+1:]. Good.
So for p>0:
`out[mid+1:mid+p+1]` length p. `h[p-1::-1]` length p.
`out[mid-1:stop:-1]` for second? Wait this is for negative second? Let's separate:
For positive second, index mid+1..mid+ln. No negative step.
For p<n-1, ln=n-1-p:
`out[mid+1:mid+ln+1]` length ln. `h[p+1:]` length ln.
Good.

Negative m=1..n-1: index mid-m.
q=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+m}). Position p first: i=p, m=1..n-1-p. Target indices mid-1, mid-2, ..., mid-(n-1-p). Coeff: 1 - h[p+m] (m=1..ln) -> h[p+1:]. Same tail.
Position p second: i+m=p -> m=p-i, m=1..p. Target indices mid-1..mid-p. Coeff: -h[i] = -h[p-m], m=1..p -> h[p-1::-1].
Thus:
- Negative first: slice descending from mid-1 down to mid-ln = p? since ln=n-1-p, mid-ln = n-1 - (n-1-p)=p. So indices mid-1, mid-2, ..., p. Need Python stop exclusive: to include p, stop=p-1 (if p>0) or None if p=0. Example p=0: indices mid-1..0 length n-1; start mid-1=n-2; stop None. Good. Coeff tail length ln=n-p-1.
- Negative second: slice descending from mid-1 down to mid-p. Include mid-p; stop=mid-p-1. If stop <0 use None. Example p=n-1: stop=mid-(n-1)-1=-1? Use None includes mid-1..0 length n-1, coeff h[p-1::-1] length n-1. Good. For p=0 skip.

So add_position_coeff code:
```
def add_position_coeff(h,p,scale,out):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1
    out[mid] += scale*(1.0 - 2.0*h[p])
    if p > 0:
        rev = h[p-1::-1]
        out[mid+1:mid+p+1] += scale*(1.0 - rev)
        stop = mid - p - 1
        if stop < 0:
            out[mid-1::-1] += scale*(-rev)
        else:
            out[mid-1:stop:-1] += scale*(-rev)
    if p < n-1:
        tail = h[p+1:]
        ln = len(tail)
        out[mid+1:mid+ln+1] += scale*(-tail)
        stop = p - 1
        if stop < 0:
            out[mid-1::-1] += scale*(1.0 - tail)
        else:
            out[mid-1:stop:-1] += scale*(1.0 - tail)
```
Need check negative second for p small p=1,n=5 mid=4, stop=4-1-1=2. slice out[3:2:-1] indices [3], length1, good. For p=2, stop=1, out[3:1:-1] indices [3,2], length2. Good. For p=3, stop=0, out[3:0:-1] indices [3,2,1], length3 (stopping exclusive at0, exclude index0? But should include down to mid-p=1, yes indices3,2,1 length3; stop0 excludes 0, good). If p=4 (n-1), stop=-1 -> None out[3::-1] indices3,2,1,0 length4, should include down to mid-p=0, yes. Good.
Negative first p=1,n=5, tail len3, p>0 stop=0, out[3:0:-1] indices3,2,1 (should indices mid-1=3 down to p=1: 3,2,1) good. p=0 stop=-1 None out[3::-1] indices3,2,1,0 (down to0) length4. good. p=3,n=5, tail len1, stop=2, out[3:2:-1] index3 (down to p=3) good. p=4 tail len0 skip.
All lengths match.

Let's write `fast_local_search` carefully with recompute. It will recompute at start. It uses `h` as input, modifies copy. It returns best exact.
```
def fast_local_search(h, deadline, top_k=None):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2.0/n
    if top_k is None: top_k = 12 if n<=500 else (10 if n<=900 else 8)
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n) ? This might alter. Maybe ensure sum but not clip? Use project. Cost okay.
    c = compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    passes=0; recompute=30; no=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
       if passes % recompute == 0:
          c = compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
       if cmax < best_c:
          best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
       limit = min(top_k, len(order))
       best_move=None; best_val = cmax + 1e-14; best_a=None; best_d=0; best_m=0; best_src=0; best_dst=0
       for oi in range(limit):
          t=int(order[oi])
          if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
          m=abs(t-mid)
          if m == 0:
             # lag0 limited
             high = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:min(25,n//2)]
             low = np.argsort(h)[:min(25,n//2)]
             for si in high:
                for dj in low:
                   if si==dj: continue
                   if h[si] <= h[dj] + 1e-9: continue
                   res = line_search_move(h,c,si,dj,cmax,dx) # cmax initial? We want compare to best_val maybe; but if cmax changed? use cmax.
                   if res[1] < best_val - 1e-14: update
             continue
          sign = 1 if t>mid else -1
          idxp=mid+m; idxn=mid-m
          for i in range(n-m):
             src = i+m if sign>0 else i
             dst = i if sign>0 else i+m
             if h[src] <= 1e-8 or h[dst] >= 1.0-1e-8: continue
             res=line_search_move(...)
             update
       if best_move:
          apply; cmax=best_val
          if cmax < best_c: ...
          no=0
       else:
          no +=1
          if no >= 3: break
       passes +=1
    # final compare current and best
    c_cur=compute_all_lags(h,n); cur=...
    if cur < best_c: return h,cur
    return best_h,best_c
```
Critical: `line_search_move` uses current `cmax` (the global current) as threshold. But in pass, if current cmax is e.g., 0.38, and a candidate improves to 0.379, good. If another candidate after that would be compared to cmax not best_val. But pass selects best_val. line_search only returns if val < cmax - tol. So ok. If best_val gets much lower, later candidates with val between best_val and cmax not needed. Good.
But if cmax variable from previous move exact but c array drift, maybe use current `cmax`. Fine.

`line_search_move` returns tuple `(d,val,a)`; if no improvement returns `(0.0, cmax, None)`? We can make it return `None` for no improvement to save. But top-level cannot use None? Yes. Define:
```
def line_search_move(h, c, src, dst, cmax, dx):
    ...
    if no quick: return None
    ...
    return best_d,best_val,a
```

def interpolation_start(h_old, n):
    if len(h_old)==n: return h_old.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h_old))
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    h_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_old)
    return project_feasible(h_new,n)
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

QUICK_FR = (...)
FULL_FR = (...)

def compute_all_lags(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * dx

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(compute_all_lags(h,n)))
```
For performance, if computing c5 for many starts, compute_all_lags then max. Good.

`project_feasible` code:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=0.5*float(n)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-15:
            break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
This may leave sum slightly off if clip after. But okay. Could final while. Maybe after clip, diff again. Use for loop 300.
```

       ... after h update, np.clip? In loop, if diff>0 adding to room might overshoot due round; we can clip? If we clip inside, diff. We can just no clip inside until end; h may exceed1 due adding diff/tot*room max? Since room sum tot, diff<tot? If h sum below target but room sum may be small; diff/tot could >1, h+>1. Need clip. Use `np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)` each iteration. Then room changes. 200 iters.
```

`initial_fallback`:
```
def initial_fallback(n, seed):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    h=1.0 - np.abs(x-1.0)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h += rng.normal(0.0,0.01,h.size)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def apply_move(h,c,src,dst,d,dx,a):
    if d <= 0: return
    mid=len(h)-1; m=abs(dst-src)
    c += a*d
    c[mid] -= 2.0*dx*d*d
    if m>0:
       add=dx*d*d
       c[mid+m] += add
       c[mid-m] += add
    h[src] -= d
    h[dst] += d
    if h[src] < 0.0:
        h[src] = 0.0
    elif h[src] > 1.0:
        h[src] = 1.0
    if h[dst] < 0.0: ...
```

def line_search_move(h,c,src,dst,cmax,dx):
    ss=h[src]; rr=1.0-h[dst]
    lim = ss if ss < rr else rr
    if lim <= 1e-10:
        return None
    a=move_a_vector(h,src,dst,dx)
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; m=abs(src-dst); idxp=mid+m; idxn=mid-m
    best_d=0.0; best_val=cmax
    for f in QUICK_FR:
       d=f*lim
       if d <= 1e-12: continue
       val=evaluate_move_with_a(c,a,d,dx,mid,idxp,idxn)
       if val < best_val - 1e-14:
          best_val=val; best_d=d
    if best_d == 0.0:
       return None
    for f in FULL_FR:
       d=f*lim
       if d <=1e-12: continue
       val=...
       if val < best_val - 1e-14:
          best_val=val; best_d=d
    return (best_d,best_val,a)

`binary_swap_search` code with secondary. Let's write carefully.
```
def binary_swap_search(h, deadline, top_k=None):
    n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2.0/float(n)
    h=np.where(h > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).astype(float).copy()
    # Ensure sum target? If not, project? For binary, if sum not n/2 due n odd? We can fix: target=n/2; extra? But n even. If not, use project_feasible then round? maybe not.
    s=h.sum(); target=0.5*n
    if abs(s-target)>1e-12:
       # adjust by flipping? too complex; use project then threshold? 
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       h=np.where(h>0.5,1.0,0.0)
    if top_k is None: top_k=binary_top_k(n)
    c=compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
    passes=0; sec_streak=0; max_secondary=100
    recompute_interval=50
    last_move=None
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
        if passes % recompute_interval == 0:
            c=compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
        order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        limit = min(top_k, 2*n-1)
        best_primary=None; best_primary_val=cmax
        best_secondary=None; best_secondary_dec=0.0
        for oi in range(limit):
            t=int(order[oi])
            m=abs(t-mid)
            if m == 0: continue
            sign=1 if t>mid else -1
            idxp=mid+m; idxn=mid-m
            # active term index is t
            for i in range(0, n-m):
                if last_move is not None:
                    src = i+m if sign>0 else i
                    dst = i if sign>0 else i+m
                    if (src,dst) == (last_move[1], last_move[0]):
                        continue
                else:
                    src=...
                if h[src] != 1.0 or h[dst] != 0.0: continue
                a=move_a_vector(h,src,dst,dx)
                val=evaluate_move_with_a(c,a,1.0,dx,mid,idxp,idxn)
                if val < best_primary_val - 1e-14:
                    best_primary=(src,dst,a,m); best_primary_val=val
                if val <= cmax + 1.0e-12:
                    # compute new active
                    add_t = dx if (t == idxp or t == idxn) else 0.0
                    new_t = c[t] + a[t] + add_t
                    dec = c[t] - new_t
                    if dec > 1e-10 and dec > best_secondary_dec + 1e-12:
                        best_secondary=(src,dst,a,m); best_secondary_dec=dec
            if time.time() > deadline - 5:
                break
        if best_primary is not None:
            src,dst,a,m=best_primary
            apply_binary_swap(h,c,src,dst,dx,a)
            cmax=best_primary_val
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
            last_move=(src,dst); sec_streak=0
            passes += 1
        elif best_secondary is not None and sec_streak < max_secondary:
            src,dst,a,m=best_secondary
            apply_binary_swap(h,c,src,dst,dx,a)
            c=compute_all_lags(h,n)
            cmax=float(c.max())
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
            last_move=(src,dst); sec_streak += 1
            passes += 1
        else:
            break
    c_cur=compute_all_lags(h,n); cur=float(c_cur.max())
    if cur < best_c:
        return h, cur
    return best_h, best_c
```

def fast_local_search(h, deadline, top_k=None):
   n=len(h); mid=n-1; dx=2.0/n
   if top_k is None: top_k=frac_top_k(n)
   h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
   c=compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax
   passes=0; recompute=30; no_improve=0; last_move=None
   while time.time() < deadline -2:
      if passes % recompute ==0:
         c=compute_all_lags(h,n); cmax=float(c.max())
         if cmax < best_c: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
      order=np.argsort(c)[::-1]; limit=min(top_k,2n-1)
      best_move=None; best_val=cmax; best_a=None; best_d=0.0; best_m=0
      for oi in range(limit):

             low=np.argsort(h)[:num]

                      d,val,a=res
                      if val < best_val - 1e-14:

             continue

            src=...; dst=...
            if h[src] <=1e-9 or h[dst] >=1.0-1e-9: continue
            if last_move and reverse: continue
            res=line_search_move(...)
            if res:
               d,val,a=res
               if val < best_val -1e-14:
                  best_val=val; best_move=(src,dst,d,a)
         if time... break

         apply_move(h,c,src,dst,d,dx,a)
         cmax=best_val
         if cmax<best_c: best_c=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
         last_move=(src,dst); no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve +=1

      passes +=1

def make_binary(h,n):
    # top n/2 entries to 1, rest0
    out=np.zeros(n)
    if n%2==0:
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n//2]
       out[idx]=1
    else:
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n//2]; out[idx]=1; out[...] =0.5? but binary search not for odd.
    return out

def interpolation_start(h_old,n):
    if len(h_old) == n:
       return h_old.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h_old))

```python
import numpy as np
import time

QUICK_FR = (0.001, 0.002, 0.01, 0.05, 0.25, 1.0)
FULL_FR = (0.001, 0.002, 0.004, 0.008, 0.015, 0.03, 0.06, 0.11, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.75, 1.0)


def compute_all_lags(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_all_lags(h, n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(300):
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def binarize(h, n, sym=False):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if sym and (n % 4 == 0):
        m = n // 2
        idx = np.arange(m)
        vals = 0.5 * (h[idx] + h[n - 1 - idx])
        top = np.argsort(vals)[::-1][: n // 4]
        out[top] = 1.0
        out[n - 1 - top] = 1.0
        return out

    target = n // 2
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:target]
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return out


def interpolation_start(h_old, n):
    if len(h_old) == n:
        return h_old.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h_old))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return project_feasible(h_new, n)


def initial_fallback(n, seed):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    h = 1.0 - np.abs(x - 1.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def add_position_coeff(h, p, scale, out):
    n = len(h)
    mid = n - 1

    out[mid] += scale * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[p])

    if p > 0:
        rev = h[p - 1::-1]

        out[mid + 1:mid + p + 1] += scale * (1.0 - rev)

        stop = mid - p - 1
        if stop < 0:
            out[mid - 1::-1] += scale * (-rev)
        else:
            out[mid - 1:stop:-1] += scale * (-rev)

    if p < n - 1:
        tail = h[p + 1:]
        ln = len(tail)

        out[mid + 1:mid + ln + 1] += scale * (-tail)

        stop = p - 1
        if stop < 0:
            out[mid - 1::-1] += scale * (1.0 - tail)
        else:
            out[mid - 1:stop:-1] += scale * (1.0 - tail)


def evaluate_move_with_a(c, a, d, dx, mid, idx_pos, idx_neg):
    if d == 0.0:
        return float(np.max(c))

    tmp = c + a * d
    tmp[mid] -= 2.0 * dx * d * d

    if idx_pos is not None:
        add = dx * d * d
        tmp[idx_pos] += add
        tmp[idx_neg] += add

    return float(np.max(tmp))


def apply_move(h, c, src, dst, d, dx, a):
    if d <= 0.0:
        return
    n = len(h)
    mid = n - 1
    m = abs(dst - src)

    c += a * d
    c[mid] -= 2.0 * dx * d * d
    if m > 0:
        add = dx * d * d
        c[mid + m] += add
        c[mid - m] += add

    h[src] -= d
    h[dst] += d

    if h[src] < 0.0:
        h[src] = 0.0
    elif h[src] > 1.0:
        h[src] = 1.0
    if h[dst] < 0.0:
        h[dst] = 0.0
    elif h[dst] > 1.0:
        h[dst] = 1.0


def apply_binary_swap(h, c, src, dst, dx, a):
    apply_move(h, c, src, dst, 1.0, dx, a)
    h[src] = 0.0
    h[dst] = 1.0


def line_search_move_ws(h, c, src, dst, cmax, dx, a_work):
    ss = h[src]
    room = 1.0 - h[dst]
    lim = ss if ss < room else room
    if lim <= 1e-10:
        return None

    n = len(h)
    mid = n - 1
    m = abs(src - dst)
    idx_pos = mid + m
    idx_neg = mid - m

    a_work.fill(0.0)
    add_position_coeff(h, dst, dx, a_work)
    add_position_coeff(h, src, -dx, a_work)

    best_d = 0.0
    best_val = cmax

    for f in QUICK_FR:
        d = f * lim
        if d <= 1e-12:
            continue
        val = evaluate_move_with_a(c, a_work, d, dx, mid, idx_pos, idx_neg)
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val = val
            best_d = d

    if best_d == 0.0:
        return None

    for f in FULL_FR:
        d = f * lim
        if d <= 1e-12:
            continue
        val = evaluate_move_with_a(c, a_work, d, dx, mid, idx_pos, idx_neg)
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val = val
            best_d = d

    return (best_d, best_val, a_work.copy())


def binary_top_k(n):
    if n <= 500:
        return 16
    if n <= 800:
        return 12
    if n <= 1200:
        return 10
    return 8


def frac_top_k(n):
    if n <= 500:
        return 12
    if n <= 800:
        return 10
    if n <= 1200:
        return 8
    return 6


def binary_swap_search(h, deadline, top_k=None):
    n = len(h)
    mid = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    h = binarize(h, n)
    if top_k is None:
        top_k = binary_top_k(n)

    c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax

    a_work = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)

    passes = 0
    recompute_interval = 50
    max_secondary = 90
    sec_streak = 0
    last_move = None

    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
        if passes % recompute_interval == 0:
            c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()

        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        limit = min(top_k, L)

        best_primary = None
        best_primary_val = cmax
        best_primary_a = None
        best_primary_m = 0

        best_secondary = None
        best_secondary_a = None
        best_secondary_m = 0
        best_secondary_dec = 0.0

        for oi in range(limit):
            if time.time() > deadline - 4:
                break
            t = int(order[oi])
            m = abs(t - mid)
            if m == 0:
                continue
            sign = 1 if t > mid else -1
            idx_pos = mid + m
            idx_neg = mid - m

            for i in range(n - m):
                if sign > 0:
                    src = i + m
                    dst = i
                else:
                    src = i
                    dst = i + m

                if last_move is not None and (src, dst) == (last_move[1], last_move[0]):
                    continue

                if h[src] != 1.0 or h[dst] != 0.0:
                    continue

                a_work.fill(0.0)
                add_position_coeff(h, dst, dx, a_work)
                add_position_coeff(h, src, -dx, a_work)

                val = evaluate_move_with_a(c, a_work, 1.0, dx, mid, idx_pos, idx_neg)

                if val < best_primary_val - 1e-14:
                    best_primary_val = val
                    best_primary = (src, dst)
                    best_primary_a = a_work.copy()
                    best_primary_m = m

                if val <= cmax + 1.0e-12:
                    add_t = dx if (t == idx_pos or t == idx_neg) else 0.0
                    new_t = c[t] + a_work[t] + add_t
                    dec = c[t] - new_t
                    if dec > 1e-10 and dec > best_secondary_dec + 1e-12:
                        best_secondary_dec = dec
                        best_secondary = (src, dst)
                        best_secondary_a = a_work.copy()
                        best_secondary_m = m

        if best_primary is not None:
            src, dst = best_primary
            apply_binary_swap(h, c, src, dst, dx, best_primary_a)
            cmax = best_primary_val
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
            last_move = (src, dst)
            sec_streak = 0
            passes += 1

        elif best_secondary is not None and sec_streak < max_secondary:
            src, dst = best_secondary
            apply_binary_swap(h, c, src, dst, dx, best_secondary_a)
            c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
            last_move = (src, dst)
            sec_streak += 1
            passes += 1

        else:
            break

    c_cur = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    cur = float(np.max(c_cur))
    if cur < best_c:
        return h, cur
    return best_h, best_c


def fast_local_search(h, deadline, top_k=None):
    n = len(h)
    mid = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if top_k is None:
        top_k = frac_top_k(n)

    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax

    a_work = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)

    passes = 0
    recompute_interval = 30
    no_improve = 0
    last_move = None

    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
        if passes % recompute_interval == 0:
            c = compute_all_lags(h, n)
            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()

        order = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        limit = min(top_k, L)

        best_move = None
        best_a = None
        best_d = 0.0
        best_val = cmax

        for oi in range(limit):
            if time.time() > deadline - 4:
                break

            t = int(order[oi])
            m = abs(t - mid)

            if m == 0:
                num = min(20, max(1, n // 2))
                high = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:num]
                low = np.argsort(h)[:num]

                for si in high:
                    si = int(si)
                    for di in low:
                        di = int(di)
                        if si == di:
                            continue
                        if h[si] <= h[di] + 1e-9:
                            continue
                        if h[si] <= 1e-9 or h[di] >= 1.0 - 1e-9:
                            continue
                        if last_move is not None and (si, di) == (last_move[1], last_move[0]):
                            continue

                        res = line_search_move_ws(h, c, si, di, cmax, dx, a_work)
                        if res is not None:
                            d, val, a = res
                            if val < best_val - 1e-14:
                                best_val = val
                                best_move = (si, di, d)
                                best_a = a
                continue

            sign = 1 if t > mid else -1

            for i in range(n - m):
                if sign > 0:
                    src = i + m
                    dst = i
                else:
                    src = i
                    dst = i + m

                if h[src] <= 1e-9 or h[dst] >= 1.0 - 1e-9:
                    continue
                if last_move is not None and (src, dst) == (last_move[1], last_move[0]):
                    continue

                res = line_search_move_ws(h, c, src, dst, cmax, dx, a_work)
                if res is not None:
                    d, val, a = res
                    if val < best_val - 1e-14:
                        best_val = val
                        best_move = (src, dst, d)
                        best_a = a

        if best_move is not None:
            src, dst, d = best_move
            apply_move(h, c, src, dst, d, dx, best_a)
            cmax = best_val
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
            last_move = (src, dst)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break

        passes += 1

    c_cur = compute_all_lags(h, n)
    cur = float(np.max(c_cur))
    if cur < best_c:
        return h, cur
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = initial_fallback(500, seed)

    n0 = len(h0)
    if n0 < 4:
        h0 = initial_fallback(500, seed)
        n0 = 500

    if n0 % 2 != 0:
        n_even = int(np.ceil(n0 / 2.0) * 2)
        h0 = interpolation_start(h0, n_even)
        n0 = n_even

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    c0 = compute_c5(h0, n0)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = c0

    h_sym = project_feasible(0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]), len(best_h))
    c_sym = compute_c5(h_sym, len(h_sym))
    if c_sym < best_c:
        best_c = c_sym
        best_h = h_sym

    n_options = [len(best_h)]
    for cand in (800, 1200):
        if cand not in n_options and len(best_h) < cand:
            n_options.append(cand)

    phase_settings = (
        (0.20, 180.0, 0.35, 300.0),
        (0.25, 200.0, 0.30, 250.0),
        (0.25, 180.0, 0.30, 180.0),
    )

    for phase_index, n in enumerate(n_options):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 90.0:
            break

        if phase_index < len(phase_settings):
            bfrac, bcap, ffrac, fcap = phase_settings[phase_index]
        else:
            bfrac, bcap, ffrac, fcap = phase_settings[-1]

        cont_starts = []
        if len(best_h) == n:
            cont_starts.append(best_h.copy())
            cont_starts.append(0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]))
        else:
            h_int = interpolation_start(best_h, n)
            cont_starts.append(h_int)
            cont_starts.append(0.5 * (h_int + h_int[::-1]))

        best_cont = None
        best_cont_c = float("inf")
        best_bin = None
        best_bin_c = float("inf")

        for s in cont_starts:
            s = project_feasible(s, n)
            cs = compute_c5(s, n)
            if cs < best_cont_c:
                best_cont_c = cs
                best_cont = s

            hb = binarize(s, n)
            cb = compute_c5(hb, n)
            if cb < best_bin_c:
                best_bin_c = cb
                best_bin = hb

            if n % 4 == 0:
                hbs = binarize(s, n, sym=True)
                cbs = compute_c5(hbs, n)
                if cbs < best_bin_c:
                    best_bin_c = cbs
                    best_bin = hbs

        if best_cont is not None and best_cont_c < best_c:
            best_c = best_cont_c
            best_h = best_cont.copy()

        btime = min(bcap, max(0.0, rem * bfrac))
        btime = min(btime, rem - 60.0)

        binary_res = None
        binary_c = float("inf")

        if btime >= 30.0 and best_bin is not None:
            bd = time.time() + btime
            binary_res, binary_c = binary_swap_search(best_bin, bd, top_k=binary_top_k(n))
            if binary_c < best_c:
                best_c = binary_c
                best_h = binary_res.copy()

        rem2 = deadline - time.time()
        ftime = min(fcap, max(0.0, rem2 * ffrac))
        ftime = min(ftime, rem2 - 20.0)

        frac_h = best_cont.copy() if best_cont is not None else best_h.copy()
        frac_c = best_cont_c if best_cont is not None else best_c

        if best_h is not None and len(best_h) == n and best_c < frac_c:
            frac_h = best_h.copy()
            frac_c = best_c

        if binary_res is not None and binary_c < frac_c:
            frac_h = binary_res.copy()
            frac_c = binary_c

        if ftime >= 30.0:
            fd = time.time() + ftime
            fh, fc = fast_local_search(frac_h, fd, top_k=frac_top_k(n))
            if fc < best_c:
                best_c = fc
                best_h = fh

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 80.0 and best_h is not None:
        n_final = len(best_h)
        fd = min(deadline - 2.0, time.time() + max(30.0, rem * 0.45))
        fh, fc = fast_local_search(best_h, fd, top_k=frac_top_k(n_final))
        if fc < best_c:
            best_c = fc
            best_h = fh

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, len(best_h))

    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```
